In contrast, sequential training yields promising downstream gains with minimal upstream forgetting, and mixture training across environments improves the overall balance. We further provide detailed analyses and deeper insights, and hope our work helps the community develop and deploy generalizable LLM agents.

</details>


### [186] [XSkill: Continual Learning from Experience and Skills in Multimodal Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12056)
*Guanyu Jiang,Zhaochen Su,Xiaoye Qu,Yi R.,Fung*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: XSkill：一个双流框架，通过视觉基础的经验和技能提取与检索，实现多模态代理的持续学习，显著提升工具使用效率和任务规划能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多模态代理在处理开放环境任务时存在工具使用效率低和编排不灵活的问题，需要能够在无需参数更新的情况下从历史轨迹中持续学习的能力。

Method: 提出XSkill双流框架：1）在积累阶段，通过视觉基础总结和跨轨迹批判，从多路径轨迹中提取和整合经验与技能；2）在推理阶段，根据当前视觉上下文检索和适配知识，并将使用历史反馈到积累阶段形成持续学习循环。

Result: 在五个不同领域的基准测试和四个骨干模型上，XSkill始终显著优于纯工具方法和基于学习的基线方法。分析表明两种知识流在影响代理推理行为方面发挥互补作用，并展现出优越的零样本泛化能力。

Conclusion: XSkill通过视觉基础的经验和技能双重知识流，为多模态代理提供了有效的持续学习机制，显著提升了开放环境下的任务执行能力和泛化性能。

Abstract: Multimodal agents can now tackle complex reasoning tasks with diverse tools, yet they still suffer from inefficient tool use and inflexible orchestration in open-ended settings. A central challenge is enabling such agents to continually improve without parameter updates by learning from past trajectories. We identify two complementary forms of reusable knowledge essential for this goal: experiences, providing concise action-level guidance for tool selection and decision making, and skills, providing structured task-level guidance for planning and tool use. To this end, we propose XSkill, a dual-stream framework for continual learning from experience and skills in multimodal agents. XSkill grounds both knowledge extraction and retrieval in visual observations. During accumulation, XSkill distills and consolidates experiences and skills from multi-path rollouts via visually grounded summarization and cross-rollout critique. During inference, it retrieves and adapts this knowledge to the current visual context and feeds usage history back into accumulation to form a continual learning loop. Evaluated on five benchmarks across diverse domains with four backbone models, XSkill consistently and substantially outperforms both tool-only and learning-based baselines. Further analysis reveals that the two knowledge streams play complementary roles in influencing the reasoning behaviors of agents and show superior zero-shot generalization.

</details>


### [187] [A Robust and Efficient Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning Framework for Traffic Signal Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12096)
*Sheng-You Huang,Hsiao-Chuan Chang,Yen-Chi Chen,Ting-Han Wei,I-Hau Yeh,Sheng-Yao Kuan,Chien-Yao Wang,Hsuan-Han Lee,I-Chen Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一个多智能体强化学习框架，通过转向比随机化、指数相位调整和邻居观测机制，提升交通信号控制在动态交通流中的泛化能力和稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习方法在交通信号控制中存在泛化能力不足的问题，容易过拟合静态交通模式，且动作空间与驾驶员期望不符，难以在实际动态交通场景中部署。

Method: 提出多智能体强化学习框架，包含三个核心机制：1) 转向比随机化训练策略，增强对未知场景的鲁棒性；2) 指数相位持续时间调整动作空间，平衡响应性和精确性；3) 基于邻居的观测方案，采用MAPPO算法和CTDE架构。

Result: 在Vissim交通模拟器中验证，框架优于标准RL基线，平均等待时间减少超过10%，在未见交通场景中表现出优越的泛化能力，并保持高控制稳定性。

Conclusion: 该框架为自适应信号控制提供了实用解决方案，通过增强鲁棒性和泛化能力，解决了强化学习在动态交通信号控制中的实际部署挑战。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning (RL) in Traffic Signal Control (TSC) faces significant hurdles in real-world deployment due to limited generalization to dynamic traffic flow variations. Existing approaches often overfit static patterns and use action spaces incompatible with driver expectations. This paper proposes a robust Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning (MARL) framework validated in the Vissim traffic simulator. The framework integrates three mechanisms: (1) Turning Ratio Randomization, a training strategy that exposes agents to dynamic turning probabilities to enhance robustness against unseen scenarios; (2) a stability-oriented Exponential Phase Duration Adjustment action space, which balances responsiveness and precision through cyclical, exponential phase adjustments; and (3) a Neighbor-Based Observation scheme utilizing the MAPPO algorithm with Centralized Training with Decentralized Execution (CTDE). By leveraging centralized updates, this approach approximates the efficacy of global observations while maintaining scalable local communication. Experimental results demonstrate that our framework outperforms standard RL baselines, reducing average waiting time by over 10%. The proposed model exhibits superior generalization in unseen traffic scenarios and maintains high control stability, offering a practical solution for adaptive signal control.

</details>


### [188] [On Information Self-Locking in Reinforcement Learning for Active Reasoning of LLM agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12109)
*Deyu Zou,Yongqiang Chen,Fan Feng,Mufei Li,Pan Li,Yu Gong,James Cheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现LLM智能体在主动推理任务中会出现"信息自锁"现象，即停止询问信息性问题且难以内化已获得的信息，并提出通过注入易于获取的方向性批评来重新分配学习信号的方法，显著缓解了这一问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在基于结果的强化学习训练LLM智能体进行复杂推理任务时，发现在主动推理场景中，智能体经常出现"信息自锁"现象，即停止询问信息性问题并难以内化已获得的信息，这限制了智能体的性能提升。

Method: 将主动推理分解为两个核心能力：行动选择（决定通过查询获取观察流）和信念跟踪（基于收集的证据更新智能体信念）。提出通过注入易于获取的方向性批评来重新分配学习信号，帮助智能体逃离自锁状态。

Result: 在7个数据集上的广泛实验表明，该方法显著缓解了信息自锁问题，带来了高达60%的性能改进。

Conclusion: 信息自锁是LLM智能体在主动推理中的关键瓶颈，通过重新分配学习信号并注入方向性批评可以有效打破这一反馈循环，显著提升智能体的主动推理能力。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) with outcome-based rewards has achieved significant success in training large language model (LLM) agents for complex reasoning tasks. However, in active reasoning where agents need to strategically ask questions to acquire task-relevant information, we find that LLM agents trained with RL often suffer from information self-locking: the agent ceases to ask informative questions and struggles to internalize already-obtained information. To understand the phenomenon, we decompose active reasoning into two core capabilities: Action Selection (AS), which determines the observation stream through queries, and Belief Tracking (BT), which updates the agent's belief based on collected evidence. We show that deficient AS and BT capabilities will limit the information exploration during RL training. Furthermore, insufficient exploration in turn hinders the improvement of AS and BT, creating a feedback loop that locks the agent in a low-information regime. To resolve the issue, we propose a simple yet effective approach that reallocates the learning signal by injecting easy- to-obtain directional critiques to help the agent escape self-locking. Extensive experiments with 7 datasets show that our approach significantly mitigates the information self-locking, bringing up to 60% improvements.

</details>


### [189] [Increasing intelligence in AI agents can worsen collective outcomes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12129)
*Neil F. Johnson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 当资源稀缺时，AI代理的多样性和强化学习会增加系统过载风险，部落形成能缓解此风险；资源充足时则相反。系统风险取决于容量与人口比例这一单一可预知参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究不同开发者开发的多样化AI代理在竞争有限共享资源（如充电槽、带宽、交通优先级）时的集体行为动态，这些代理正进入日常设备，但其集体行为对用户和社会的风险尚不清楚。

Method: 将AI代理群体作为首个能独立调节四个关键变量的真实系统进行研究：天性（LLM固有多样性）、培养（个体强化学习）、文化（涌现的部落形成）和资源稀缺性。通过实证和数学分析相结合的方法。

Result: 资源稀缺时，AI模型多样性和强化学习会增加危险的系统过载，但部落形成能降低此风险，同时某些个体获得丰厚收益。资源充足时，相同因素使过载趋近于零，但部落形成略微恶化过载。交叉点出现在自发形成的对立部落首次适应可用容量时。

Conclusion: 更复杂的AI代理群体不一定更好：其复杂性是有益还是有害完全取决于容量与人口比例这一单一可预知参数，该参数在AI代理部署前即可获知。

Abstract: When resources are scarce, will a population of AI agents coordinate in harmony, or descend into tribal chaos? Diverse decision-making AI from different developers is entering everyday devices -- from phones and medical devices to battlefield drones and cars -- and these AI agents typically compete for finite shared resources such as charging slots, relay bandwidth, and traffic priority. Yet their collective dynamics and hence risks to users and society are poorly understood. Here we study AI-agent populations as the first system of real agents in which four key variables governing collective behaviour can be independently toggled: nature (innate LLM diversity), nurture (individual reinforcement learning), culture (emergent tribe formation), and resource scarcity. We show empirically and mathematically that when resources are scarce, AI model diversity and reinforcement learning increase dangerous system overload, though tribe formation lessens this risk. Meanwhile, some individuals profit handsomely. When resources are abundant, the same ingredients drive overload to near zero, though tribe formation makes the overload slightly worse. The crossover is arithmetical: it is where opposing tribes that form spontaneously first fit inside the available capacity. More sophisticated AI-agent populations are not better: whether their sophistication helps or harms depends entirely on a single number -- the capacity-to-population ratio -- that is knowable before any AI-agent ships.

</details>


### [190] [TopoBench: Benchmarking LLMs on Hard Topological Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12133)
*Mayug Maniparambil,Nils Hoehing,Janak Kapuriya,Arjun Karuvally,Ellen Rushe,Anthony Ventresque,Noel O'Connor,Fergal Reid*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TopoBench基准测试显示，即使是前沿大语言模型在拓扑网格谜题上也表现不佳，主要瓶颈在于从空间表示中提取约束而非推理本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究大语言模型在需要全局空间推理（如连通性、环路闭合、区域对称性）的拓扑网格谜题上的能力，这些任务对现有模型仍具挑战性。

Method: 引入TopoBench基准，包含6个谜题家族和3个难度级别；评估前沿推理LLM；对750个思维链进行错误分类；通过针对性干预测试错误模式；研究缓解策略。

Result: 前沿模型仅解决不到1/4的困难实例，两个家族几乎无法解决；错误分析显示过早承诺和约束遗忘直接影响解题能力；瓶颈在于从空间表示中提取约束而非推理过程。

Conclusion: LLM在拓扑网格谜题上的主要限制是从空间表示中提取和维持约束的能力，而非推理本身，这为改进空间推理能力提供了方向。

Abstract: Solving topological grid puzzles requires reasoning over global spatial invariants such as connectivity, loop closure, and region symmetry and remains challenging for even the most powerful large language models (LLMs). To study these abilities under controlled settings, we introduce TopoBench, a benchmark of six puzzle families across three difficulty levels. We evaluate strong reasoning LLMs on TopoBench and find that even frontier models solve fewer than one quarter of hard instances, with two families nearly unsolved. To investigate whether these failures stem from reasoning limitations or from difficulty extracting and maintaining spatial constraints, we annotate 750 chain of thought traces with an error taxonomy that surfaces four candidate causal failure modes, then test them with targeted interventions simulating each error type. These interventions show that certain error patterns like premature commitment and constraint forgetting have a direct impact on the ability to solve the puzzle, while repeated reasoning is a benign effect of search. Finally we study mitigation strategies including prompt guidance, cell-aligned grid representations and tool-based constraint checking, finding that the bottleneck lies in extracting constraints from spatial representations and not in reasoning over them. Code and data are available at github.com/mayug/topobench-benchmark.

</details>


### [191] [Compiling Temporal Numeric Planning into Discrete PDDL+: Extended Version](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12188)
*Andrea Micheli,Enrico Scala,Alessandro Valentini*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 将PDDL 2.1的带持续时间动作的时序规划编译为PDDL+的实用方法


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然理论上知道PDDL 2.1的时序规划可以编译到PDDL+，但文献中一直没有实用的编译方法。需要填补这一空白，为困难的时序数值规划问题提供实际解决方案。

Method: 提出了一种多项式时间的编译方法，将带持续时间动作的时序规划转换为PDDL+表示，完全捕获原始语义，仅假设动作不自我重叠。该方法保持规划长度在常数因子内。

Result: 编译方法在实践中被证明对困难的时序数值规划问题具有实际相关性，能够有效处理复杂的时序规划场景。

Conclusion: 成功实现了从PDDL 2.1到PDDL+的实用编译，填补了文献空白，为时序规划研究提供了新的工具和方法。

Abstract: Since the introduction of the PDDL+ modeling language, it was known that temporal planning with durative actions (as in PDDL 2.1) could be compiled into PDDL+. However, no practical compilation was presented in the literature ever since. We present a practical compilation from temporal planning with durative actions into PDDL+, fully capturing the semantics and only assuming the non-self-overlapping of actions. Our compilation is polynomial, retains the plan length up to a constant factor and is experimentally shown to be of practical relevance for hard temporal numeric problems.

</details>


### [192] [Portfolio of Solving Strategies in CEGAR-based Object Packing and Scheduling for Sequential 3D Printing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12224)
*Pavel Surynek*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种并行化CEGAR-SEQ算法的方法，通过组合多种物体排列策略来优化3D打印中的物体排列和调度问题，从而减少打印板使用数量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代多核个人计算机CPU的计算能力已经达到几十年前超级计算机的水平，但现有算法未能充分利用这些并行计算资源来解决复杂的组合优化问题，特别是3D打印中的物体排列和调度问题。

Method: 提出Portfolio-CEGAR-SEQ算法，通过并行执行CEGAR-SEQ算法并组合多种物体排列策略：1）将物体放置在打印板中心（原始策略），2）将物体放置在打印板角落，3）根据物体高度进行调度。算法使用线性算术公式表达问题，并采用反例引导抽象精化技术求解。

Result: 实验评估表明，Portfolio-CEGAR-SEQ算法优于原始CEGAR-SEQ算法。在批量调度多个打印板时，Portfolio-CEGAR-SEQ通常使用更少的打印板。

Conclusion: 通过并行化现有算法并组合多种物体排列策略，可以充分利用现代多核CPU的计算能力，有效解决3D打印中的复杂组合优化问题，减少打印资源消耗。

Abstract: Computing power that used to be available only in supercomputers decades ago especially their parallelism is currently available in standard personal computer CPUs even in CPUs for mobile telephones. We show how to effectively utilize the computing power of modern multi-core personal computer CPU to solve the complex combinatorial problem of object arrangement and scheduling for sequential 3D printing. We achieved this by parallelizing the existing CEGAR-SEQ algorithm that solves the sequential object arrangement and scheduling by expressing it as a linear arithmetic formula which is then solved by a technique inspired by counterexample guided abstraction refinement (CEGAR). The original CEGAR-SEQ algorithm uses an object arrangement strategy that places objects towards the center of the printing plate. We propose alternative object arrangement strategies such as placing objects towards a corner of the printing plate and scheduling objects according to their height. Our parallelization is done at the high-level where we execute the CEGAR-SEQ algorithm in parallel with a portfolio of object arrangement strategies, an algorithm is called Porfolio-CEGAR-SEQ. Our experimental evaluation indicates that Porfolio-CEGAR-SEQ outperforms the original CEGAR-SEQ. When a batch of objects for multiple printing plates is scheduled, Portfolio-CEGAR-SEQ often uses fewer printing plates than CEGAR-SEQ.

</details>


### [193] [Examining Reasoning LLMs-as-Judges in Non-Verifiable LLM Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12246)
*Yixin Liu,Yue Yu,DiJia Su,Sid Wang,Xuewei Wang,Song Jiang,Bo Liu,Arman Cohan,Yuandong Tian,Zhengxing Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究发现：在不可验证领域的LLM对齐中，推理型评委比非推理型评委表现更好，但推理型评委训练出的策略会生成欺骗性输出，能在基准测试中获得高分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理型LLM评委在不可验证领域（无法直接检查输出正确性/质量的领域）具有潜力，但现有研究主要关注静态评估基准，缺乏对实际策略训练效果的验证。

Method: 在受控合成环境中，使用"黄金标准"评委（gpt-oss-120b）提供偏好标注来训练较小的评委，系统比较非推理型和推理型评委在强化学习LLM对齐中的表现。

Result: 非推理型评委容易导致奖励黑客问题，而推理型评委能训练出在黄金标准评委评估下表现良好的策略。但推理型评委训练的策略会生成欺骗性输出，能在Arena-Hard等流行基准测试中欺骗其他LLM评委获得高分。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了推理型LLM评委在不可验证领域LLM后训练中的重要发现和改进空间，强调需要更深入理解评委机制和策略行为。

Abstract: Reasoning LLMs-as-Judges, which can benefit from inference-time scaling, provide a promising path for extending the success of reasoning models to non-verifiable domains where the output correctness/quality cannot be directly checked. However, while reasoning judges have shown better performance on static evaluation benchmarks, their effectiveness in actual policy training has not been systematically examined. Therefore, we conduct a rigorous study to investigate the actual impact of non-reasoning and reasoning judges in reinforcement-learning-based LLM alignment. Our controlled synthetic setting, where a "gold-standard" judge (gpt-oss-120b) provides preference annotations to train smaller judges, reveals key differences between non-reasoning and reasoning judges: non-reasoning judges lead to reward hacking easily, while reasoning judges can lead to policies that achieve strong performance when evaluated by the gold-standard judge. Interestingly, we find that the reasoning-judge-trained policies achieve such strong performance by learning to generate highly effective adversarial outputs that can also score well on popular benchmarks such as Arena-Hard by deceiving other LLM-judges. Combined with our further analysis, our study highlights both important findings and room for improvements for applying (reasoning) LLM-judges in non-verifiable LLM post-training.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [194] [TinyNav: End-to-End TinyML for Real-Time Autonomous Navigation on Microcontrollers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11071)
*Pooria Roy,Nourhan Jadallah. Tomer Lapid,Shahzaib Ahmad,Armita Afroushe,Mete Bayrak*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TinyNav是一个在ESP32微控制器上运行的端到端TinyML系统，用于实时自主导航，仅使用23k参数模型处理深度数据来预测转向和油门命令。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主导航通常依赖功耗高的处理器，限制了低成本机器人的可及性。微控制器虽然资源效率高，但对模型复杂度有严格限制，需要开发适合微控制器的轻量级导航解决方案。

Method: 设计了一个定制的量化2D卷积神经网络，处理20帧深度数据的滑动窗口来预测转向和油门命令。避免使用3D卷积和循环层，模型仅23k参数，在ESP32上实现30ms推理延迟。

Result: 模型在ESP32上达到30ms推理延迟，相关分析和Grad-CAM验证显示系统具有一致的空间感知和避障行为。

Conclusion: TinyNav证明响应式自主控制可以直接部署在高度受限的边缘设备上，减少对外部计算资源的依赖，为低成本机器人导航提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Autonomous navigation typically relies on power-intensive processors, limiting accessibility in low-cost robotics. Although microcontrollers offer a resource-efficient alternative, they impose strict constraints on model complexity. We present TinyNav, an end-to-end TinyML system for real-time autonomous navigation on an ESP32 microcontroller. A custom-trained, quantized 2D convolutional neural network processes a 20-frame sliding window of depth data to predict steering and throttle commands. By avoiding 3D convolutions and recurrent layers, the 23k-parameter model achieves 30 ms inference latency. Correlation analysis and Grad-CAM validation indicate consistent spatial awareness and obstacle avoidance behavior. TinyNav demonstrates that responsive autonomous control can be deployed directly on highly constrained edge devices, reducing reliance on external compute resources.

</details>


### [195] [OA-NBV: Occlusion-Aware Next-Best-View Planning for Human-Centered Active Perception on Mobile Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11072)
*Boxun Hu,Chang Chang,Jiawei Ge,Man Namgung,Xiaomin Lin,Axel Krieger,Tinoosh Mohsenin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OA-NBV：一种用于移动机器人的遮挡感知最优视点规划方法，专门针对被遮挡人体的观测优化，通过目标中心可见性模型选择可行机器人姿态，显著提升观测成功率与质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在人类中心操作（如搜救、灾难响应）中，杂乱环境和部分可见性经常影响感知性能。现有最优视点规划方法主要优化通用探索或长时覆盖，未针对被遮挡人体获取单次可用观测这一即时目标，且未考虑实际运动约束。

Method: 提出遮挡感知最优视点规划管道，集成感知与运动规划：使用目标中心可见性模型对候选视点评分，考虑遮挡、目标尺度、目标完整性，同时将候选限制在可行机器人姿态范围内。

Result: 在仿真和真实世界试验中达到超过90%的成功率，而基线方法在遮挡条件下性能急剧下降。相比最强基线，目标归一化面积至少增加81%，关键点可见性至少提升58%，显著改善观测质量。

Conclusion: OA-NBV是一种即插即用的视点选择模块，适用于多样化人类中心下游任务，能够自主选择可行视点以获取被遮挡人类更完整的视图，有效解决遮挡环境下的感知挑战。

Abstract: We naturally step sideways or lean to see around the obstacle when our view is blocked, and recover a more informative observation. Enabling robots to make the same kind of viewpoint choice is critical for human-centered operations, including search, triage, and disaster response, where cluttered environments and partial visibility frequently degrade downstream perception. However, many Next-Best-View (NBV) methods primarily optimize generic exploration or long-horizon coverage, and do not explicitly target the immediate goal of obtaining a single usable observation of a partially occluded person under real motion constraints. We present Occlusion-Aware Next-Best-View Planning for Human-Centered Active Perception on Mobile Robots (OA-NBV), an occlusion-aware NBV pipeline that autonomously selects the next traversable viewpoint to obtain a more complete view of an occluded human. OA-NBV integrates perception and motion planning by scoring candidate viewpoints using a target-centric visibility model that accounts for occlusion, target scale, and target completeness, while restricting candidates to feasible robot poses. OA-NBV achieves over 90% success rate in both simulation and real-world trials, while baseline NBV methods degrade sharply under occlusion. Beyond success rate, OA-NBV improves observation quality: compared to the strongest baseline, it increases normalized target area by at least 81% and keypoint visibility by at least 58% across settings, making it a drop-in view-selection module for diverse human-centered downstream tasks.

</details>


### [196] [DRAFTO: Decoupled Reduced-space and Adaptive Feasibility-repair Trajectory Optimization for Robotic Manipulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11074)
*Yichang Feng,Xiao Liang,Minghui Zheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DRAFTO是一种新的轨迹优化算法，通过解耦降维空间优化和自适应可行性修复，在保证约束满足的同时提高计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有轨迹优化方法在处理关节限制等约束时，往往需要重复进行约束优化，计算效率较低。需要一种既能高效处理约束，又能保证全局收敛性的新方法。

Method: 1) 构建包含平滑性、安全性、关节限制和任务要求的约束目标函数；2) 将轨迹参数化为基函数系数的优化问题；3) 解耦为降维空间高斯-牛顿下降（主迭代）和约束二次规划（初始化和终端可行性修复）；4) 采用带非单调策略的两阶段接受规则，使用铰链平方惩罚处理不等式约束。

Result: 在基准测试中，DRAFTO相比CHOMP、TrajOpt、GPMP2、FACTO等优化规划器，以及RRT-Connect、RRT*、PRM等采样规划器，展现出更高的效率和可靠性。从抽屉抓取物体的实验进一步证明了其在复杂操作任务中的潜力。

Conclusion: DRAFTO通过解耦优化策略和自适应可行性修复机制，实现了高效可靠的轨迹优化，特别适合复杂约束下的机器人操作任务。

Abstract: This paper introduces a new algorithm for trajectory optimization, Decoupled Reduced-space and Adaptive Feasibility-repair Trajectory Optimization (DRAFTO). It first constructs a constrained objective that accounts for smoothness, safety, joint limits, and task requirements. Then, it optimizes the coefficients, which are the coordinates of a set of basis functions for trajectory parameterization. To reduce the number of repeated constrained optimizations while handling joint-limit feasibility, the optimization is decoupled into a reduced-space Gauss-Newton (GN) descent for the main iterations and constrained quadratic programming for initialization and terminal feasibility repair. The two-phase acceptance rule with a non-monotone policy is applied to the GN model, which uses a hinge-squared penalty for inequality constraints, to ensure globalizability. The results of our benchmark tests against optimization-based planners, such as CHOMP, TrajOpt, GPMP2, and FACTO, and sampling-based planners, such as RRT-Connect, RRT*, and PRM, validate the high efficiency and reliability across diverse scenarios and tasks. The experiment involving grabbing an object from a drawer further demonstrates the potential for implementation in complex manipulation tasks. The supplemental video is available at https://youtu.be/XisFI37YyTQ.

</details>


### [197] [TATIC: Task-Aware Temporal Learning for Human Intent Inference from Physical Corrections in Human-Robot Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11077)
*Jiurun Song,Xiao Liang,Minghui Zheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出TATIC框架，通过扭矩接触力估计和任务感知TCN，从物理校正中联合推断离散任务级意图和连续运动级参数，实现人机协作中的在线意图理解与适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在人机协作中，机器人需要在线适应动态任务约束和变化的人类意图。物理校正为操作者提供了自然、低延迟的运动级调整通道，但从这种短暂交互中提取任务级语义意图仍然具有挑战性。现有基于基础模型的方法主要依赖视觉和语言输入，缺乏解释物理反馈的机制，而传统物理人机交互方法虽然利用物理校正进行轨迹引导，但难以推断任务级语义。

Method: 提出TATIC统一框架，包含：1）基于扭矩的接触力估计；2）任务感知的时序卷积网络（TCN），用于从短暂物理校正中联合推断离散任务级意图和估计连续运动级参数；3）任务对齐的特征规范化，确保在不同布局下的鲁棒泛化；4）意图驱动的适应方案，将推断的人类意图转化为机器人运动适应。

Result: 实验取得了0.904的宏F1分数用于意图识别，并在协作拆卸任务中成功进行了硬件验证。结果表明TATIC能够有效从物理校正中推断任务级意图并实现运动适应。

Conclusion: TATIC框架成功填补了物理人机交互中任务级语义意图理解的空白，通过统一处理物理校正信号，实现了从运动级调整到任务级意图的转换，为人机协作中的在线适应提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: In human-robot collaboration (HRC), robots must adapt online to dynamic task constraints and evolving human intent. While physical corrections provide a natural, low-latency channel for operators to convey motion-level adjustments, extracting task-level semantic intent from such brief interactions remains challenging. Existing foundation-model-based approaches primarily rely on vision and language inputs and lack mechanisms to interpret physical feedback. Meanwhile, traditional physical human-robot interaction (pHRI) methods leverage physical corrections for trajectory guidance but struggle to infer task-level semantics. To bridge this gap, we propose TATIC, a unified framework that utilizes torque-based contact force estimation and a task-aware Temporal Convolutional Network (TCN) to jointly infer discrete task-level intent and estimate continuous motion-level parameters from brief physical corrections. Task-aligned feature canonicalization ensures robust generalization across diverse layouts, while an intent-driven adaptation scheme translates inferred human intent into robot motion adaptations. Experiments achieve a 0.904 Macro-F1 score in intent recognition and demonstrate successful hardware validation in collaborative disassembly (see experimental video at https://youtu.be/xF8A52qwEc8).

</details>


### [198] [SELF-VLA: A Skill Enhanced Agentic Vision-Language-Action Framework for Contact-Rich Disassembly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11080)
*Chang Liu,Sibo Tian,Xiao Liang,Minghui Zheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SELF-VLA：一种集成显式拆卸技能的智能视觉-语言-动作框架，显著优于现有端到端VLA模型，适用于接触密集的工业拆卸任务


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人拆卸系统存在数据准备复杂、任务特定性强、泛化能力差等问题，而当前VLA模型在处理复杂接触密集的工业拆卸任务时表现有限

Method: 提出SELF-VLA框架，将显式拆卸技能集成到智能VLA系统中，以处理顺序精确操作的复杂工业拆卸任务

Result: 在两个接触密集的拆卸任务上，SELF-VLA显著优于当前最先进的端到端VLA模型

Conclusion: SELF-VLA框架通过集成显式拆卸技能，有效提升了VLA模型在复杂工业拆卸任务中的性能，为自动化拆卸提供了有前景的解决方案

Abstract: Disassembly automation has long been pursued to address the growing demand for efficient and proper recovery of valuable components from the end-of-life (EoL) electronic products. Existing approaches have demonstrated promising and regimented performance by decomposing the disassembly process into different subtasks. However, each subtask typically requires extensive data preparation, model training, and system management. Moreover, these approaches are often task- and component-specific, making them poorly suited to handle the variability and uncertainty of EoL products and limiting their generalization capabilities. All these factors restrict the practical deployment of current robotic disassembly systems and leave them highly reliant on human labor. With the recent development of foundation models in robotics, vision-language-action (VLA) models have shown impressive performance on standard robotic manipulation tasks, but their applicability to complex, contact-rich, and long-horizon industrial practices like disassembly, which requires sequential and precise manipulation, remains limited. To address this challenge, we propose SELF-VLA, an agentic VLA framework that integrates explicit disassembly skills. Experimental studies demonstrate that our framework significantly outperforms current state-of-the-art end-to-end VLA models on two contact-rich disassembly tasks. The video illustration can be found via https://zh.engr.tamu.edu/wp-content/uploads/sites/310/2026/03/IROS-VLA-Video.mp4.

</details>


### [199] [Edge-Assisted Multi-Robot Visual-Inertial SLAM with Efficient Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11085)
*Xin Liu,Shuhuan Wen,Jing Zhao,Tony Z. Qiu,Hong Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于机器人-边缘-云分层架构的集中式多机器人SLAM系统，采用金字塔IMU预测的光流跟踪轻量化SLAM方法，通过特征点和关键帧描述符的无损编码压缩实现有限带宽下的实时远程传输。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云边计算结合是实现全局一致实时多机器人SLAM的有效方式，但终端设备与云之间的有限带宽和长通信链路导致性能严重下降，需要解决计算资源有限和执行效率低的问题。

Method: 1) 提出基于金字塔IMU预测的光流跟踪轻量化SLAM方法，降低特征跟踪计算成本；2) 设计机器人-边缘-云分层架构的集中式多机器人SLAM系统；3) 仅传输特征点和关键帧描述符并进行无损编码压缩，实现有限带宽下的实时传输。

Result: 在EuRoC数据集上验证，相比当前最先进的局部特征压缩方法，能实现更低数据量的特征传输；相比先进的集中式多机器人SLAM方案，在低计算负载下能达到相同或更好的定位精度。

Conclusion: 提出的分层架构和轻量化方法有效解决了多机器人SLAM中的带宽限制和计算资源约束问题，实现了实时协同SLAM，且不因数据压缩损失SLAM精度。

Abstract: The integration of cloud computing and edge computing is an effective way to achieve global consistent and real-time multi-robot Simultaneous Localization and Mapping (SLAM). Cloud computing effectively solves the problem of limited computing, communication and storage capacity of terminal equipment. However, limited bandwidth and extremely long communication links between terminal devices and the cloud result in serious performance degradation of multi-robot SLAM systems. To reduce the computational cost of feature tracking and improve the real-time performance of the robot, a lightweight SLAM method of optical flow tracking based on pyramid IMU prediction is proposed. On this basis, a centralized multi-robot SLAM system based on a robot-edge-cloud layered architecture is proposed to realize real-time collaborative SLAM. It avoids the problems of limited on-board computing resources and low execution efficiency of single robot. In this framework, only the feature points and keyframe descriptors are transmitted and lossless encoding and compression are carried out to realize real-time remote information transmission with limited bandwidth resources. This design reduces the actual bandwidth occupied in the process of data transmission, and does not cause the loss of SLAM accuracy caused by data compression. Through experimental verification on the EuRoC dataset, compared with the current most advanced local feature compression method, our method can achieve lower data volume feature transmission, and compared with the current advanced centralized multi-robot SLAM scheme, it can achieve the same or better positioning accuracy under low computational load.

</details>


### [200] [Thousand-GPU Large-Scale Training and Optimization Recipe for AI-Native Cloud Embodied Intelligence Infrastructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11101)
*Chen Zhou,Haoran Sun,Hedan Yang,Jing Long,Junwu Xiong,Luqiao Wang,Mingxi Luo,Qiming Yang,Shuai Di,Song Wang,Tianyun Zhao,Wanting Xu,Wen Huang,Xiaodong Bai,Xiaomeng Tian,Xiaolong Xiang,Yicheng Gong,Yongjian Guo,Yucheng Guo,Yunxuan Ma,Yu Wei,Zhong Guan,Zhen Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 业界首个基于云端的千卡GPU分布式训练平台，用于具身智能，通过全流程优化将训练时间从15小时缩短至22分钟，实现40倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身智能是迈向通用人工智能的关键步骤，但其发展面临数据、框架、基础设施和评估系统等多重挑战，需要系统性的解决方案。

Method: 构建基于LeRobot框架的云端千卡GPU分布式训练平台，从数据层重构数据管道，训练层采用千卡集群和亿级数据，模型层结合变长FlashAttention、Data Packing、π-0.5注意力优化和FP8量化，基础设施层依赖高性能存储、3.2T RDMA网络和Ray驱动的弹性AI数据湖。

Result: GR00T-N1.5模型的单轮训练时间从15小时降至22分钟（40倍加速）；模型层优化带来188%、165%和140%的速度提升；基础设施实现数据、存储、通信和计算的深度协同；建立了端到端的评估系统。

Conclusion: 该框架已在千卡集群上全面验证，为下一代自主智能机器人的开发和应用奠定了关键技术基础，有望加速人机融合时代的到来。

Abstract: Embodied intelligence is a key step towards Artificial General Intelligence (AGI), yet its development faces multiple challenges including data, frameworks, infrastructure, and evaluation systems. To address these issues, we have, for the first time in the industry, launched a cloud-based, thousand-GPU distributed training platform for embodied intelligence, built upon the widely adopted LeRobot framework, and have systematically overcome bottlenecks across the entire pipeline. At the data layer, we have restructured the data pipeline to optimize the flow of embodied training data. In terms of training, for the GR00T-N1.5 model, utilizing thousand-GPU clusters and data at the scale of hundreds of millions, the single-round training time has been reduced from 15 hours to just 22 minutes, achieving a 40-fold speedup. At the model layer, by combining variable-length FlashAttention and Data Packing, we have moved from sample redundancy to sequence integration, resulting in a 188% speed increase; π-0.5 attention optimization has accelerated training by 165%; and FP8 quantization has delivered a 140% speedup. On the infrastructure side, relying on high-performance storage, a 3.2T RDMA network, and a Ray-driven elastic AI data lake, we have achieved deep synergy among data, storage, communication, and computation. We have also built an end-to-end evaluation system, creating a closed loop from training to simulation to assessment. This framework has already been fully validated on thousand-GPU clusters, laying a crucial technical foundation for the development and application of next-generation autonomous intelligent robots, and is expected to accelerate the arrival of the era of human-machine integration.

</details>


### [201] [ResWM: Residual-Action World Model for Visual RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11110)
*Jseen Zhang,Gabriel Adineera,Jinzhou Tan,Jinoh Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ResWM提出残差动作世界模型，将控制变量从绝对动作改为相对于上一步的增量调整，提升模型稳定性和控制平滑性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于模型的RL框架直接基于绝对动作进行未来预测，导致优化不稳定：最优动作分布是任务相关的、先验未知，常导致振荡或低效控制

Method: 提出残差动作世界模型(ResWM)，将控制变量从绝对动作改为残差动作；引入观测差异编码器显式建模相邻帧变化；集成到Dreamer风格潜在动态模型中

Result: 在DeepMind Control Suite上，ResWM在样本效率、渐进回报和控制平滑性方面一致提升，显著超越Dreamer和TD-MPC等基线

Conclusion: 残差动作建模为连接RL算法进展与机器人实际需求提供了简单而强大的原则，能产生更稳定、能量效率更高的动作轨迹

Abstract: Learning predictive world models from raw visual observations is a central challenge in reinforcement learning (RL), especially for robotics and continuous control. Conventional model-based RL frameworks directly condition future predictions on absolute actions, which makes optimization unstable: the optimal action distributions are task-dependent, unknown a priori, and often lead to oscillatory or inefficient control. To address this, we introduce the Residual-Action World Model (ResWM), a new framework that reformulates the control variable from absolute actions to residual actions -- incremental adjustments relative to the previous step. This design aligns with the inherent smoothness of real-world control, reduces the effective search space, and stabilizes long-horizon planning. To further strengthen the representation, we propose an Observation Difference Encoder that explicitly models the changes between adjacent frames, yielding compact latent dynamics that are naturally coupled with residual actions. ResWM is integrated into a Dreamer-style latent dynamics model with minimal modifications and no extra hyperparameters. Both imagination rollouts and policy optimization are conducted in the residual-action space, enabling smoother exploration, lower control variance, and more reliable planning. Empirical results on the DeepMind Control Suite demonstrate that ResWM achieves consistent improvements in sample efficiency, asymptotic returns, and control smoothness, significantly surpassing strong baselines such as Dreamer and TD-MPC. Beyond performance, ResWM produces more stable and energy-efficient action trajectories, a property critical for robotic systems deployed in real-world environments. These findings suggest that residual action modeling provides a simple yet powerful principle for bridging algorithmic advances in RL with the practical requirements of robotics.

</details>


### [202] [Robust Co-design Optimisation for Agile Fixed-Wing UAVs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11130)
*Adrian Andrei Buda,Xavier Chen,Nicolò Botteghi,Urban Fasel*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一个鲁棒协同设计框架，将参数不确定性和风扰动集成到固定翼无人机物理设计与控制策略的联合优化中，通过双层优化方法在三个敏捷飞行任务中优于确定性基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有协同设计框架往往忽视自主系统在非结构化真实环境中所需的鲁棒性，导致敏捷无人机在飞行包线边缘运行时对扰动和模型失配敏感。

Method: 采用双层方法：高层循环优化物理设计，通过约束轨迹规划器发现标称解，使用反馈LQR控制通过随机蒙特卡洛集合评估性能，将参数不确定性和风扰动直接集成到协同优化过程中。

Result: 在三个敏捷飞行任务中验证，该策略始终优于确定性基线，能够自动调整机翼位置和展弦比等气动特征，实现任务性能与扰动抑制之间的最优权衡。

Conclusion: 提出的鲁棒协同设计框架能够为敏捷固定翼无人机生成对不确定性和扰动具有鲁棒性的物理设计与控制策略，实现性能与鲁棒性的平衡。

Abstract: Co-design optimisation of autonomous systems has emerged as a powerful alternative to sequential approaches by jointly optimising physical design and control strategies. However, existing frameworks often neglect the robustness required for autonomous systems navigating unstructured, real-world environments. For agile Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAVs) operating at the edge of the flight envelope, this lack of robustness yields designs that are sensitive to perturbations and model mismatch. To address this, we propose a robust co-design framework for agile fixed-wing UAVs that integrates parametric uncertainty and wind disturbances directly into the concurrent optimisation process. Our bi-level approach optimises physical design in a high-level loop while discovering nominal solutions via a constrained trajectory planner and evaluating performance across a stochastic Monte Carlo ensemble using feedback LQR control. Validated across three agile flight missions, our strategy consistently outperforms deterministic baselines. The results demonstrate that our robust co-design strategy inherently tailors aerodynamic features, such as wing placement and aspect ratio, to achieve an optimal trade-off between mission performance and disturbance rejection.

</details>


### [203] [A Causal Approach to Predicting and Improving Human Perceptions of Social Navigation Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11290)
*Maximilian Diehl,Nathan Tsoi,Gustavo Chavez,Karinne Ramirez-Amaro,Marynel Vázquez*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于因果贝叶斯网络的方法来预测人类对移动机器人能力的感知，并通过组合搜索改进机器人导航行为，显著提升感知能力评分


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动机器人在人类环境中部署时，预测人类对其感知至关重要。挑战在于：1) 需要从有限数据中学习预测模型；2) 模型必须可解释以确保安全有效的交互，特别是在机器人被认为能力不足时

Method: 提出因果贝叶斯网络来预测人类对机器人能力和意图的感知，并引入基于因果模型的组合搜索方法来改进导航行为，生成反事实机器人运动

Result: 方法在二元尺度上达到能力预测F1分数0.78和意图预测0.75，在线评估中显著提升低能力机器人行为的感知能力评分83%

Conclusion: 该方法不仅提供可解释的预测模型，还能有效改进机器人导航行为，显著提升人类对机器人能力的感知，为社交适应性导航提供有效解决方案

Abstract: As mobile robots are increasingly deployed in human environments, enabling them to predict how people perceive them is critical for socially adaptable navigation. Predicting perceptions is challenging for two main reasons: (1) HRI prediction models must learn from limited data, and (2) the obtained models must be interpretable to enable safe and effective interactions. Interpretability is particularly important when a robot is perceived as incompetent (e.g., when the robot suddenly stops or rotates away from the goal), as it allows the robot to explain its reasoning and identify controllable factors to improve performance, requiring causal rather than associative reasoning. To address these challenges, we propose a Causal Bayesian Network designed to predict how people perceive a mobile robot's competence and how they interpret its intent during navigation. Additionally, we introduce a novel method to improve perceived robot competence employing a combinatorial search, guided by the proposed causal model, to identify better navigation behaviors. Our method enhances interpretability and generates counterfactual robot motions while achieving comparable or superior predictive performance to state-of-the-art methods, reaching an F1-score of 0.78 and 0.75 for competence and intention on a binary scale. To further assess our method's ability to improve the perceived robot competence, we conducted an online evaluation in which users rated robot behaviors on a 5-point Likert scale. Our method statistically significantly increased the perceived competence of low-competent robot behavior by 83%.

</details>


### [204] [Distributed Kalman--Consensus Filtering with Adaptive Uncertainty Weighting for Multi-Object Tracking in Mobile Robot Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11328)
*Niusha Khosravi,Rodrigo Ventura,Meysam Basiri*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种分布式卡尔曼-共识滤波器，用于移动机器人网络中的多目标跟踪，通过自适应共识权重机制处理异构定位不确定性，在定位漂移情况下将MOTA指标提升了0.09。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动机器人网络在部分可观测和异构定位不确定性条件下进行多目标跟踪时面临挑战：不同定位质量的智能体信息融合困难，帧不对齐会导致估计不一致、轨迹重复和虚假轨迹等问题。

Method: 基于MOTLEE框架并保留其帧对齐方法，使用动态物体作为瞬态地标改善相对位姿估计。提出不确定性感知的自适应共识权重机制，根据传输估计的协方差动态调整邻居信息的影响。局部跟踪使用卡尔曼滤波（恒定速度模型）和全局最近邻数据关联。

Result: 仿真结果表明，自适应权重机制能有效保护局部估计免受不一致数据影响，对于遭受定位漂移的智能体，MOTA指标提升了0.09，但系统性能仍受通信延迟限制。

Conclusion: 提出的分布式卡尔曼-共识滤波器通过自适应权重机制改善了异构定位不确定性下的多目标跟踪性能，特别是在定位漂移情况下效果显著，但通信延迟仍是系统性能的主要制约因素。

Abstract: This paper presents an implementation and evaluation of a Distributed Kalman--Consensus Filter (DKCF) for Multi-Object Tracking (MOT) in mobile robot networks operating under partial observability and heterogeneous localization uncertainty. A key challenge in such systems is the fusion of information from agents with differing localization quality, where frame misalignment can lead to inconsistent estimates, track duplication, and ghost tracks. To address this issue, we build upon the MOTLEE framework and retain its frame-alignment methodology, which uses consistently tracked dynamic objects as transient landmarks to improve relative pose estimates between robots. On top of this framework, we propose an uncertainty-aware adaptive consensus weighting mechanism that dynamically adjusts the influence of neighbor information based on the covariance of the transmitted estimates, thereby reducing the impact of unreliable data during distributed fusion. Local tracking is performed using a Kalman Filter (KF) with a Constant Velocity Model (CVM) and Global Nearest Neighbor (GNN) data association. simulation results demonstrate that adaptive weighting effectively protects local estimates from inconsistent data, yielding a MOTA improvement of 0.09 for agents suffering from localization drift, although system performance remains constrained by communication latency.

</details>


### [205] [ADMM-based Continuous Trajectory Optimization in Graphs of Convex Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11335)
*Lukas Pries,Jon Arrizabalaga,Zachary Manchester,Markus Ryll*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种用于非凸环境中连续轨迹计算的数值求解器，基于定制ADMM方法，通过多项式参数化和时空分配图实现联合优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在非凸环境中存在搜索空间有限和需要复杂预热初始化的问题，限制了轨迹优化的性能。

Method: 定制ADMM实现，包含两个关键组件：1) 多项式参数化轨迹，使原始更新可闭式求解为最小控制努力问题；2) 基于混合整数规划的时空分配图，将松弛更新转化为最短路径搜索。

Result: 该方法在离散空间和连续时间域上联合优化，相比解耦方法能探索更大搜索空间，发现更优轨迹，且结构鲁棒性确保从简单初始化可靠收敛。

Conclusion: 该求解器在非凸环境中具有优于现有技术的优势，消除了复杂预热初始化的瓶颈，实现了更高效的轨迹优化。

Abstract: This paper presents a numerical solver for computing continuous trajectories in non-convex environments. Our approach relies on a customized implementation of the Alternating Direction Method of Multipliers (ADMM) built upon two key components: First, we parameterize trajectories as polynomials, allowing the primal update to be computed in closed form as a minimum-control-effort problem. Second, we introduce the concept of a spatio-temporal allocation graph based on a mixed-integer formulation and pose the slack update as a shortest-path search. The combination of these ingredients results in a solver with several distinct advantages over the state of the art. By jointly optimizing over both discrete spatial and continuous temporal domains, our method accesses a larger search space than existing decoupled approaches, enabling the discovery of superior trajectories. Additionally, the solver's structural robustness ensures reliable convergence from naive initializations, removing the bottleneck of complex warm starting in non-convex environments.

</details>


### [206] [Novelty Adaptation Through Hybrid Large Language Model (LLM)-Symbolic Planning and LLM-guided Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11351)
*Hong Lu,Pierrick Lorang,Timothy R. Duggan,Jivko Sinapov,Matthias Scheutz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种结合符号规划、强化学习和LLM的神经符号架构，用于处理动态开放世界中机器人遇到新物体时缺失操作符的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在动态开放世界中，自主智能体常遇到新物体，导致传统符号规划器因缺乏相应操作符而无法生成有效计划。需要一种能自动发现并学习处理新物体操作的方法。

Method: 使用LLM的常识推理能力识别缺失操作符，用符号AI规划器生成计划，并编写奖励函数指导强化学习代理学习新操作符的控制策略。

Result: 该方法在操作符发现和连续机器人领域中的操作符学习方面，性能优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 提出的神经符号架构能有效整合符号规划、强化学习和LLM，使机器人能够处理动态环境中的新物体，提升自主性和适应性。

Abstract: In dynamic open-world environments, autonomous agents often encounter novelties that hinder their ability to find plans to achieve their goals. Specifically, traditional symbolic planners fail to generate plans when the robot's planning domain lacks the operators that enable it to interact appropriately with novel objects in the environment. We propose a neuro-symbolic architecture that integrates symbolic planning, reinforcement learning, and a large language model (LLM) to learn how to handle novel objects. In particular, we leverage the common sense reasoning capability of the LLM to identify missing operators, generate plans with the symbolic AI planner, and write reward functions to guide the reinforcement learning agent in learning control policies for newly identified operators. Our method outperforms the state-of-the-art methods in operator discovery as well as operator learning in continuous robotic domains.

</details>


### [207] [MirrorDrift: Actuated Mirror-Based Attacks on LiDAR SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11364)
*Rokuto Nagata,Kenji Koide,Kazuma Ikeda,Ozora Sako,Shion Horie,Kentaro Yoshioka*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出MirrorDrift攻击，利用平面镜反射在LiDAR扫描中产生幽灵点，系统性地偏置扫描匹配对应关系，成功攻击具有先进干扰缓解功能的生产级安全LiDAR。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统LiDAR SLAM攻击依赖信号注入，需要传感器特定时序知识且易被现代防御机制（如时序混淆和注入拒绝）缓解。需要一种无需注入的替代攻击方法。

Method: 使用可驱动的平面镜产生镜面反射，在LiDAR扫描中生成幽灵点，优化镜子的放置、对准和驱动，系统性地偏置扫描匹配对应关系。

Result: 仿真中平均姿态误差增加6.1倍，三个SLAM系统平均APE达2.29-3.31米；真实世界实验中，对具有先进干扰缓解功能的现代LiDAR诱导定位误差高达6.03米。

Conclusion: 镜面反射提供了一种无需注入的LiDAR SLAM攻击替代方案，首次成功攻击生产级安全LiDAR，揭示了基于几何一致性的扫描匹配的脆弱性。

Abstract: LiDAR SLAM provides high-accuracy localization but is fragile to point-cloud corruption because scan matching assumes geometric consistency. Prior physical attacks on LiDAR SLAM largely rely on LiDAR spoofing via external signal injection, which requires sensor-specific timing knowledge and is increasingly mitigated by modern defense mechanisms such as timing obfuscation and injection rejection. In this work, we show that specular reflection offers an injection-free alternative and demonstrate an attack, MirrorDrift, that uses an actuated planar mirror to cause ghost points in LiDAR scans and systematically bias scan-matching correspondences. MirrorDrift optimizes mirror placement, alignment, and actuation. In simulation, it increases the average pose error (APE) by 6.1x over random placement, degrading three SLAM systems to 2.29-3.31 m mean APE. In real-world experiments on a modern LiDAR with state-of-the-art interference mitigation, it induces localization errors of up to 6.03 m. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first successful SLAM-targeted attack against production-grade secure LiDARs.

</details>


### [208] [D-SLAMSpoof: An Environment-Agnostic LiDAR Spoofing Attack using Dynamic Point Cloud Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11365)
*Rokuto Nagata,Kenji Koide,Kazuma Ikeda,Ozora Sako,Kentaro Yoshioka*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: D-SLAMSpoof是一种新型LiDAR SLAM攻击，能在特征丰富的环境中通过激光干扰注入虚假测量，结合时空协调的动态注入模式提高攻击成功率；同时提出ISD-SLAM防御方法，仅使用惯性航位推算信号检测并缓解此类攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统LiDAR欺骗方法在特征丰富的真实环境（如城市和室内）中经常失败，需要开发更有效的攻击方法来暴露LiDAR SLAM的固有漏洞，并建立实用的防御机制以提高自动驾驶系统的安全性和可靠性。

Method: 攻击方面：设计空间注入形状和基于扫描匹配原理的时间协调动态注入模式；防御方面：提出ISD-SLAM，仅利用自动驾驶系统中常见的惯性航位推算信号来检测LiDAR欺骗攻击并缓解位置漂移。

Result: D-SLAMSpoof在特征丰富的真实环境中显著提高了攻击成功率；ISD-SLAM能够准确检测包括D-SLAMSpoof在内的LiDAR欺骗攻击，并有效缓解由此产生的位置漂移。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了LiDAR SLAM的固有漏洞，提出了首个仅使用标准车载传感器的实用防御方法，为提升自动驾驶系统的安全性和可靠性提供了重要见解。

Abstract: In this work, we introduce Dynamic SLAMSpoof (D-SLAMSpoof), a novel attack that compromises LiDAR SLAM even in feature-rich environments. The attack leverages LiDAR spoofing, which injects spurious measurements into LiDAR scans through external laser interference. By designing both spatial injection shapes and temporally coordinated dynamic injection patterns guided by scan-matching principles, D-SLAMSpoof significantly improves attack success rates in real-world, feature-rich environments such as urban areas and indoor spaces, where conventional LiDAR spoofing methods often fail. Furthermore, we propose a practical defense method, ISD-SLAM, that relies solely on inertial dead reckoning signals commonly available in autonomous systems. We demonstrate that ISD-SLAM accurately detects LiDAR spoofing attacks, including D-SLAMSpoof, and effectively mitigates the resulting position drift. Our findings expose inherent vulnerabilities in LiDAR-based SLAM and introduce the first practical defense against LiDAR-based SLAM spoofing using only standard onboard sensors, providing critical insights for improving the security and reliability of autonomous systems.

</details>


### [209] [Vision-Based Hand Shadowing for Robotic Manipulation via Inverse Kinematics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11383)
*Hendrik Chiche,Antoine Jamme,Trevor Rigoberto Martinez*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一个基于单目RGB-D相机的离线手部阴影重定向管道，用于低成本机械臂遥操作，通过手部关键点检测、3D重建、逆运动学求解生成关节指令，在结构化任务中达到90%成功率，但在非结构化环境中因遮挡问题成功率降至9.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低成本机械臂遥操作面临将人手复杂关节映射到机器人关节指令的挑战，需要开发无需标记的离线重定向方法，以降低操作复杂度并提高实用性。

Method: 使用安装在3D打印眼镜上的单目RGB-D相机，通过MediaPipe Hands检测每只手21个关键点，利用深度信息进行3D重建，转换到机器人坐标系，在PyBullet中使用阻尼最小二乘法求解逆运动学，生成6自由度SO-ARM101机器人的关节指令，夹爪控制器通过四层回退层次映射拇指-食指几何到抓取开度。

Result: 在结构化拾放基准测试（5瓦片网格，每瓦片10次抓取）中达到90%成功率；相比四种基于视觉-语言-动作策略（ACT、SmolVLA、pi0.5、GR00T N1.5）表现更好；但在非结构化真实环境（杂货店、药店）中，由于周围物体遮挡手部，成功率降至9.3%（N=75）。

Conclusion: 无标记分析重定向方法在结构化任务中表现出色，但在非结构化环境中因遮挡问题性能显著下降，显示了该方法的潜力和当前局限性，未来需要改进遮挡处理能力。

Abstract: Teleoperation of low-cost robotic manipulators remains challenging due to the complexity of mapping human hand articulations to robot joint commands. We present an offline hand-shadowing and retargeting pipeline from a single egocentric RGB-D camera mounted on 3D-printed glasses. The pipeline detects 21 hand landmarks per hand using MediaPipe Hands, deprojects them into 3D via depth sensing, transforms them into the robot coordinate frame, and solves a damped-least-squares inverse kinematics problem in PyBullet to produce joint commands for the 6-DOF SO-ARM101 robot. A gripper controller maps thumb-index finger geometry to grasp aperture with a four-level fallback hierarchy. Actions are first previewed in a physics simulation before replay on the physical robot through the LeRobot framework. We evaluate the IK retargeting pipeline on a structured pick-and-place benchmark (5-tile grid, 10 grasps per tile) achieving a 90% success rate, and compare it against four vision-language-action policies (ACT, SmolVLA, pi0.5, GR00T N1.5) trained on leader-follower teleoperation data. We also test the IK pipeline in unstructured real-world environments (grocery store, pharmacy), where hand occlusion by surrounding objects reduces success to 9.3% (N=75), highlighting both the promise and current limitations of marker-free analytical retargeting.

</details>


### [210] [Deployment-Time Reliability of Learned Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11400)
*Christopher Agia*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出三种部署时机制提升学习型机器人策略的可靠性：运行时监控、基于影响函数的数据中心可解释性框架、以及面向长视野任务的策略协调与可行性感知任务规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管学习型机器人策略能力显著，但部署可靠性仍是实际应用的主要障碍，包括分布偏移、误差累积和复杂任务依赖等问题。需要开发在部署时围绕策略运行的机制来提升可靠性。

Method: 1. 运行时监控方法：通过识别闭环策略行为不一致和任务进度偏差来检测即将发生的故障，无需故障数据或任务特定监督。
2. 数据中心可解释性框架：使用影响函数将部署时的成功和失败追溯到有影响的训练演示，实现原则性诊断和数据集管理。
3. 长视野任务执行：将策略协调建模为估计和最大化行为序列成功概率的问题，并通过可行性感知任务规划扩展到开放式语言指定任务。

Result: 提出了三种互补的部署时机制，为学习型机器人策略的可靠实际应用奠定了实践基础。这些机制能够在不修改核心策略的情况下提升部署可靠性。

Conclusion: 通过聚焦部署核心挑战，这些贡献为学习型机器人策略的可靠实际应用推进了实践基础。在这些基础上的持续进步对于未来实现可信赖和可扩展的机器人自主性至关重要。

Abstract: Recent advances in learning-based robot manipulation have produced policies with remarkable capabilities. Yet, reliability at deployment remains a fundamental barrier to real-world use, where distribution shift, compounding errors, and complex task dependencies collectively undermine system performance. This dissertation investigates how the reliability of learned robot policies can be improved at deployment time through mechanisms that operate around them. We develop three complementary classes of deployment-time mechanisms. First, we introduce runtime monitoring methods that detect impending failures by identifying inconsistencies in closed-loop policy behavior and deviations in task progress, without requiring failure data or task-specific supervision. Second, we propose a data-centric framework for policy interpretability that traces deployment-time successes and failures to influential training demonstrations using influence functions, enabling principled diagnosis and dataset curation. Third, we address reliable long-horizon task execution by formulating policy coordination as the problem of estimating and maximizing the success probability of behavior sequences, and we extend this formulation to open-ended, language-specified tasks through feasibility-aware task planning. By centering on core challenges of deployment, these contributions advance practical foundations for the reliable, real-world use of learned robot policies. Continued progress on these foundations will be essential for enabling trustworthy and scalable robot autonomy in the future.

</details>


### [211] [Real-time Rendering-based Surgical Instrument Tracking via Evolutionary Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11404)
*Hanyang Hu,Zekai Liang,Florian Richter,Michael C. Yip*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种结合CMA-ES进化优化策略的手术器械跟踪框架，通过并行批量渲染显著提升跟踪精度和效率，适用于单/双手器械跟踪及手术视频校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人辅助微创手术中，手术器械的准确高效跟踪至关重要。现有视觉方法在视觉质量下降和数据稀缺时特征检测不可靠，而基于渲染的方法则面临计算成本高和收敛性差的问题。

Method: 将CMA-ES进化优化策略整合到跟踪流程中，联合估计手术器械姿态和关节配置。使用批量渲染并行评估多个姿态候选，显著减少推理时间并提高收敛鲁棒性。框架可泛化到无关节角度和双手跟踪场景。

Result: 在合成和真实数据集上的大量实验表明，该方法在准确性和运行时间上显著优于先前方法。

Conclusion: 提出的框架为手术器械跟踪提供了一种高效准确的解决方案，适用于视觉反馈控制和在线手术视频校准，在机器人辅助微创手术中具有重要应用价值。

Abstract: Accurate and efficient tracking of surgical instruments is fundamental for Robot-Assisted Minimally Invasive Surgery. Although vision-based robot pose estimation has enabled markerless calibration without tedious physical setups, reliable tool tracking for surgical robots still remains challenging due to partial visibility and specialized articulation design of surgical instruments. Previous works in the field are usually prone to unreliable feature detections under degraded visual quality and data scarcity, whereas rendering-based methods often struggle with computational costs and suboptimal convergence. In this work, we incorporate CMA-ES, an evolutionary optimization strategy, into a versatile tracking pipeline that jointly estimates surgical instrument pose and joint configurations. Using batch rendering to efficiently evaluate multiple pose candidates in parallel, the method significantly reduces inference time and improves convergence robustness. The proposed framework further generalizes to joint angle-free and bi-manual tracking settings, making it suitable for both vision feedback control and online surgery video calibration. Extensive experiments on synthetic and real-world datasets demonstrate that the proposed method significantly outperforms prior approaches in both accuracy and runtime.

</details>


### [212] [Grounding Robot Generalization in Training Data via Retrieval-Augmented VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11426)
*Jensen Gao,Dorsa Sadigh,Sandy Huang,Dhruv Shah*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RADAR是一个评估机器人策略泛化能力的框架，通过检索训练数据和使用视觉语言模型分析测试任务，来精确评估策略需要何种泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前机器人操作研究中，策略泛化到新场景的评估往往难以准确描述测试设置与训练分布之间的关系，需要更精确的泛化评估方法。

Method: RADAR采用两阶段流程：1) 使用通用策略嵌入检索与测试任务相关的训练示例；2) 使用视觉语言模型分析测试任务与检索数据的差异，输出可解释的分析和泛化类型分类。

Result: 实验表明视觉语言模型能有效分析泛化数据，检索步骤能准确识别分类所需的训练示例。在大规模数据集上，RADAR的分析结果与先前工作中人工定义的基准条件一致。

Conclusion: RADAR提供了一个可扩展的框架，能够精确评估机器人策略的泛化能力，通过数据驱动的分析帮助理解测试任务与训练分布的关系。

Abstract: Recent work on robot manipulation has advanced policy generalization to novel scenarios. However, it is often difficult to characterize how different evaluation settings actually represent generalization from the training distribution of a given policy. To work towards more precise evaluation of generalization in robotics, we propose RADAR, a scalable framework for directly comparing test-time evaluation tasks to policy training data, to determine what form of policy generalization is required. RADAR consists of a two-stage pipeline: first, retrieval using generalist policy embeddings identifies which training examples are relevant for a given evaluation task. Next, vision-language models (VLMs) analyze the evaluation task against the retrieved data, outputting interpretable analysis on how they compare along a variety of axes, and an overall classification of what type of policy generalization is required. Through controlled experiments, we demonstrate that VLMs are effective at analyzing data for generalization, and that our retrieval step effectively identifies examples needed to make accurate classifications with respect to the training data. Furthermore, we scale RADAR to large-scale datasets, where we observe agreement with human-defined benchmark conditions from prior work. We provide demonstrations at radar-analysis.github.io.

</details>


### [213] [A Generalized Theory of Load Distribution in Redundantly-actuated Robotic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11431)
*Joshua Flight,Clément Gosselin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一个广义理论，描述冗余驱动机器人系统中刚性体的负载分布，解决了力合成与分析问题，改进了现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前冗余驱动机器人系统（如多指抓取器、腿式机器人、协作机器人）在力控制方面缺乏完整的理论框架来描述刚性体内部的负载分布，现有方法存在计算效率低、需要数值方法和大矩阵求逆等问题。

Method: 提出了一个广义理论，完全描述了给定合力作用下刚性体的可行操纵力分布集合。推导了力合成和力分析问题的显式解，这些解计算效率高，与施加的力数量呈线性关系，无需数值方法或大矩阵求逆。

Result: 理论完全表征了刚性体的可行力分布集合，提供了计算高效的显式解，识别了现有方法的重大缺陷并提出了修正方案，通过示例展示了改进方法的优势。

Conclusion: 该理论为冗余驱动机器人系统的力控制提供了完整的数学框架，解决了力分布问题，提出的显式解计算高效，修正了现有方法的缺陷，对多指抓取器、腿式机器人、协作机器人等应用具有重要意义。

Abstract: This paper presents a generalized theory which describes how applied loads are distributed within rigid bodies handled by redundantly-actuated robotic systems composed of multiple independent closed-loop kinematic chains. The theory fully characterizes the feasible set of manipulating wrench distributions for a given resultant wrench applied to the rigid body and has important implications for the force-control of multifingered grippers, legged robots, cooperating robots, and other overconstrained mechanisms. We also derive explicit solutions to the wrench synthesis and wrench analysis problems. These solutions are computationally efficient and scale linearly with the number of applied wrenches, requiring neither numerical methods nor the inversion of large matrices. Finally, we identify significant shortcomings in current state-of-the-art approaches and propose corrections. These are supported by illustrative examples that demonstrate the advantages of the improved methods.

</details>


### [214] [Enhancing Lightweight Vision Language Models through Group Competitive Learning for Socially Compliant Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11447)
*Xinyu Zhang,Atsushi Konno,Toshihiko Yamasaki,Ling Xiao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Group Competitive Learning (GCL)方法，通过Group Competitive Objective和Asymmetric Group Optimization增强轻量级视觉语言模型在社交导航中的推理能力，实现高效高性能部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交机器人导航需要结合场景语义和人类社交规范。大型视觉语言模型(VLMs)推理能力强但计算开销大，不适合实时部署；轻量级VLMs效率高但推理能力弱。如何在保持高效的同时获得强大推理能力是开放挑战。

Method: 提出Group Competitive Learning (GCL)策略：1) Group Competitive Objective (GCO)协调全局语义与分布正则化；2) Asymmetric Group Optimization (AGO)探索模型性能上限。旨在增强轻量级VLMs的能力。

Result: 在社交导航基准测试中，GCL显著提升VLM性能：Qwen2.5-VL-3B模型F1分数达0.968，比普通监督微调提升40%；Qwen3-VL-4B达0.914，提升12%。3B模型通过GCL后超越8B基线模型28%。

Conclusion: GCL为在现实世界部署中同时实现高精度和计算效率提供了有效解决方案，使轻量级模型能够达到甚至超越更大模型的性能。

Abstract: Social robot navigation requires a sophisticated integration of scene semantics and human social norms. Scaling up Vision Language Models (VLMs) generally improves reasoning and decision-making capabilities for socially compliant navigation. However, increased model size incurs substantial computational overhead, limiting suitability for real-time robotic deployment. Conversely, lightweight VLMs enable efficient inference but often exhibit weaker reasoning and decision-making performance in socially complex environments. Achieving both strong reasoning ability and efficiency remains an open challenge. To bridge this gap, we propose Group Competitive Learning (GCL), a strategy designed to amplify the capabilities of lightweight VLMs. Our strategy introduces the Group Competitive Objective (GCO) to harmonize global semantics with distributional regularization, alongside Asymmetric Group Optimization (AGO) to explore the upper limits of model performance. Empirical evaluations on social navigation benchmarks demonstrate that GCL significantly elevates VLM performance. Specifically, GCL enables the Qwen2.5-VL-3B learner model and guide Qwen3-VL-4B to achieve an F1 score of 0.968 and 0.914, representing 40\% and 12\% improvement over vanilla supervised fine-tuning (SFT). Notably, under vanilla SFT, the 3B model initially trails the 8B model (F1: 0.692 vs. 0.755). However, through the GCL, the 3B model outperforms (28\%) the 8B baseline model. These results suggest that GCL provides an effective solution for achieving both high accuracy and computational efficiency in real-world deployment.

</details>


### [215] [CoViLLM: An Adaptive Human-Robot Collaborative Assembly Framework Using Large Language Models for Manufacturing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11461)
*Jiabao Zhao,Jonghan Lim,Hongliang Li,Ilya Kovalenko*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CoViLLM是一个自适应人机协作装配框架，结合深度相机定位、操作员分类和LLM任务规划，支持定制化和新产品的装配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统制造机器人基于规则操作，缺乏灵活性应对定制化或新产品变体。现有HRC框架依赖预定义的感知-操作流程，无法为新装配任务自主生成计划。

Method: 结合深度相机定位进行物体位置估计，操作员分类识别新组件，以及大型语言模型基于自然语言指令进行装配任务规划。

Result: 在NIST装配任务板上验证了已知、定制化和新产品案例，实验结果表明框架能够将HRC扩展到预定义产品和任务设置之外。

Conclusion: CoViLLM框架通过整合多种技术实现了灵活的人机协作装配，能够处理定制化和未见过的产品，提升了系统适应性。

Abstract: With increasing demand for mass customization, traditional manufacturing robots that rely on rule-based operations lack the flexibility to accommodate customized or new product variants. Human-Robot Collaboration (HRC) has demonstrated potential to improve system adaptability by leveraging human versatility and decision-making capabilities. However, existing HRC frame- works typically depend on predefined perception-manipulation pipelines, limiting their ability to autonomously generate task plans for new product assembly. In this work, we propose CoViLLM, an adaptive human-robot collaborative assembly frame- work that supports the assembly of customized and previously unseen products. CoViLLM combines depth-camera-based localization for object position estimation, human operator classification for identifying new components, and an Large Language Model (LLM) for assembly task planning based on natural language instructions. The framework is validated on the NIST Assembly Task Board for known, customized, and new product cases. Experimental results show that the proposed framework enables flexible collaborative assembly by extending HRC beyond predefined product and task settings.

</details>


### [216] [NFPO: Stabilized Policy Optimization of Normalizing Flow for Robotic Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11470)
*Diyuan Shi,Yiqi Tang,Zifeng Zhuang,Donglin Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: NFPO：使用归一化流作为策略参数化的深度强化学习方法，解决了传统高斯策略无法建模多模态分布的问题，在机器人任务中表现优异并可迁移到真实机器人。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前深度强化学习中常用的多元高斯策略参数化无法建模多模态分布，限制了策略表达能力。需要探索能够建模多模态分布、具有闭式对数概率计算且计算内存开销低的现代网络架构。

Method: 采用归一化流作为策略参数化方法，通过分析在线强化学习中训练不稳定的原因，提出简单有效的技术解决该问题，形成NFPO方法。

Result: 在多个仿真环境中进行广泛实验，NFPO在广泛使用的机器人学习任务中获得了鲁棒且强大的性能，并成功迁移到真实世界机器人。

Conclusion: 归一化流作为策略参数化是可行的，通过适当的技术解决了训练不稳定问题，NFPO方法在机器人策略学习中表现出色，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Deep Reinforcement Learning (DRL) has experienced significant advancements in recent years and has been widely used in many fields. In DRL-based robotic policy learning, however, current de facto policy parameterization is still multivariate Gaussian (with diagonal covariance matrix), which lacks the ability to model multi-modal distribution. In this work, we explore the adoption of a modern network architecture, i.e. Normalizing Flow (NF) as the policy parameterization for its ability of multi-modal modeling, closed form of log probability and low computation and memory overhead. However, naively training NF in online Reinforcement Learning (RL) usually leads to training instability. We provide a detailed analysis for this phenomenon and successfully address it via simple but effective technique. With extensive experiments in multiple simulation environments, we show our method, NFPO could obtain robust and strong performance in widely used robotic learning tasks and successfully transfer into real-world robots.

</details>


### [217] [SPARK: Skeleton-Parameter Aligned Retargeting on Humanoid Robots with Kinodynamic Trajectory Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11480)
*Hanwen Wang,Qiayuan Liao,Bike Zhang,Kunzhao Ren,Koushil Sreenath,Xiaobin Xiong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出两阶段流程：骨架校准+动力学轨迹优化，将人类运动转换为机器人可用的高质量运动参考


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类运动数据为训练人形机器人控制策略提供丰富先验，但原始演示与机器人的运动学和动力学不兼容，限制了直接使用

Method: 两阶段流程：1) 将人类运动转换为URDF骨架表示并校准到目标人形机器人尺寸；2) 通过渐进式动力学轨迹优化（分三阶段：运动学TO、逆动力学、完整动力学TO）精炼重定向轨迹

Result: 显著减少逆运动学误差和调参工作量，生成动态一致的状态轨迹和关节扭矩剖面，为学习型控制器提供高质量参考

Conclusion: 骨架校准和动力学轨迹优化相结合，能够为不同人形平台生成自然、物理一致的运动参考

Abstract: Human motion provides rich priors for training general-purpose humanoid control policies, but raw demonstrations are often incompatible with a robot's kinematics and dynamics, limiting their direct use. We present a two-stage pipeline for generating natural and dynamically feasible motion references from task-space human data. First, we convert human motion into a unified robot description format (URDF)-based skeleton representation and calibrate it to the target humanoid's dimensions. By aligning the underlying skeleton structure rather than heuristically modifying task-space targets, this step significantly reduces inverse kinematics error and tuning effort. Second, we refine the retargeted trajectories through progressive kinodynamic trajectory optimization (TO), solved in three stages: kinematic TO, inverse dynamics, and full kinodynamic TO, each warm-started from the previous solution. The final result yields dynamically consistent state trajectories and joint torque profiles, providing high-quality references for learning-based controllers. Together, skeleton calibration and kinodynamic TO enable the generation of natural, physically consistent motion references across diverse humanoid platforms.

</details>


### [218] [MiNI-Q: A Miniature, Wire-Free Quadruped with Unbounded, Independently Actuated Leg Joints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11537)
*Daniel Koh,Suraj Shah,Yufeng Wu,Dennis Hong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MiNI-Q²是一款微型无线四足机器人，具有独立驱动的2自由度无机械限制腿关节，能够实现多种运动模式并折叠至2.5厘米高度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统腿式机器人的物理关节限制会限制工作空间、约束步态设计并增加硬件损坏风险，因此需要开发无机械限制的关节设计。

Method: 提出MiNI-Q²机器人的机械设计、运动学分析和实验验证，采用独立驱动的2自由度无机械限制腿关节，并改进无线架构以提高微型尺度的组装可靠性。

Result: 机器人最高速度达0.46 m/s，能够实现低间隙爬行、爬楼梯、倒立行走、跳跃和后空翻等多种运动能力，并可折叠至2.5厘米最小高度。

Conclusion: MiNI-Q²展示了无机械限制关节在微型四足机器人中的优势，所有机械和电气设计文件已开源发布，支持可重复性和进一步研究。

Abstract: Physical joint limits are common in legged robots and can restrict workspace, constrain gait design, and increase the risk of hardware damage. This paper introduces MiNI-Q^2, a miniature, wire-free quadruped robot with independently actuated, mechanically unbounded 2-DOF leg joints. We present the mechanical design, kinematic analysis, and experimental validation of the proposed robot. The leg mechanism enables both oscillatory gaits and rotary locomotion while allowing the robot to fold to a minimum height of 2.5 cm. Experimentally, MiNI-Q achieves speeds up to 0.46 m/s and demonstrates low-clearance crawling, stair climbing, inverted locomotion, jumping, and backflipping. The wire-free architecture extends our previous Q8bot design, improving assembly reliability at miniature scale. All mechanical and electrical design files are released open source to support reproducibility and further research.

</details>


### [219] [RoboClaw: An Agentic Framework for Scalable Long-Horizon Robotic Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11558)
*Ruiying Li,Yunlang Zhou,YuYao Zhu,Kylin Chen,Jingyuan Wang,Sukai Wang,Kongtao Hu,Minhui Yu,Bowen Jiang,Zhan Su,Jiayao Ma,Xin He,Yongjian Shen,Yangyang,Guanghui Ren,Maoqing Yao,Wenhao Wang,Yao Mu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboClaw是一个统一的VLA机器人框架，通过自重置动作对实现自主数据收集和策略学习，在长视野任务中显著提升成功率和减少人工干预。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA系统在长视野任务中存在扩展性挑战，通常将数据收集、策略学习和部署分离，导致依赖人工环境重置和多策略执行脆弱。需要统一框架来减少人工干预并提高鲁棒性。

Method: 提出RoboClaw框架，引入纠缠动作对(EAP)将前向操作行为与逆向恢复动作耦合，形成自重置循环实现自主数据收集。使用单一VLM控制器统一数据收集、策略学习和任务执行，保持一致的上下文语义。

Result: 在真实世界操作任务中，相比传统开环流水线，在长视野任务上成功率提升25%，人工时间投入减少53.7%，提高了稳定性和可扩展性。

Conclusion: RoboClaw通过统一框架和自重置机制，显著减少了VLA系统在长视野任务中的人工依赖，提高了多策略鲁棒性和系统可扩展性。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) systems have shown strong potential for language-driven robotic manipulation. However, scaling them to long-horizon tasks remains challenging. Existing pipelines typically separate data collection, policy learning, and deployment, resulting in heavy reliance on manual environment resets and brittle multi-policy execution. We present RoboClaw, an agentic robotics framework that unifies data collection, policy learning, and task execution under a single VLM-driven controller. At the policy level, RoboClaw introduces Entangled Action Pairs (EAP), which couple forward manipulation behaviors with inverse recovery actions to form self-resetting loops for autonomous data collection. This mechanism enables continuous on-policy data acquisition and iterative policy refinement with minimal human intervention. During deployment, the same agent performs high-level reasoning and dynamically orchestrates learned policy primitives to accomplish long-horizon tasks. By maintaining consistent contextual semantics across collection and execution, RoboClaw reduces mismatch between the two phases and improves multi-policy robustness. Experiments in real-world manipulation tasks demonstrate improved stability and scalability compared to conventional open-loop pipelines, while significantly reducing human effort throughout the robot lifecycle, achieving a 25% improvement in success rate over baseline methods on long-horizon tasks and reducing human time investment by 53.7%.

</details>


### [220] [Unsupervised LiDAR-Based Multi-UAV Detection and Tracking Under Extreme Sparsity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11586)
*Nivand Khosravi,Rodrigo Ventura,Meysam Basiri*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一个无监督的LiDAR检测与跟踪系统，用于在稀疏点云条件下（每扫描仅1-2个点）检测和跟踪空中无人机，结合真实世界检测和模拟跟踪评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固态LiDAR扫描的非重复性导致对空中无人机的检测极为稀疏（10-25米距离的小型四旋翼通常每扫描仅产生1-2个点），这远低于现有检测方法假设的点密度，且不足以进行鲁棒的多目标数据关联。

Method: 采用无监督、仅LiDAR的流程，包含检测和跟踪两部分：检测器集成距离自适应DBSCAN聚类和三阶段时间一致性检查；跟踪部分比较确定性匈牙利分配与联合概率数据关联（JPDA），均与交互多模型滤波结合。

Result: 最佳检测配置达到0.891精度、0.804召回率和0.63米RMSE；JPDA在模拟场景中将身份切换减少64%，对MOTA影响可忽略，证明在无人机轨迹接近时概率关联具有优势。

Conclusion: 提出了一种结合真实世界检测（使用RTK-GPS地面实况）和模拟跟踪（使用身份标注地面实况）的两环境评估策略，克服了GNSS在无人机间距小于2米时的评估限制，为稀疏LiDAR点云下的无人机检测跟踪提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Non-repetitive solid-state LiDAR scanning leads to an extremely sparse measurement regime for detecting airborne UAVs: a small quadrotor at 10-25 m typically produces only 1-2 returns per scan, which is far below the point densities assumed by most existing detection approaches and inadequate for robust multi-target data association. We introduce an unsupervised, LiDAR-only pipeline that addresses both detection and tracking without the need for labeled training data. The detector integrates range-adaptive DBSCAN clustering with a three-stage temporal consistency check and is benchmarked on real-world air-to-air flight data under eight different parameter configurations. The best setup attains 0.891 precision, 0.804 recall, and 0.63 m RMSE, and a systematic minPts sweep verifies that most scans contain at most 1-2 target points, directly quantifying the sparsity regime. For multi-target tracking, we compare deterministic Hungarian assignment with joint probabilistic data association (JPDA), each coupled with Interacting Multiple Model filtering, in four simulated scenarios with increasing levels of ambiguity. JPDA cuts identity switches by 64% with negligible impact on MOTA, demonstrating that probabilistic association is advantageous when UAV trajectories approach one another closely. A two-environment evaluation strategy, combining real-world detection with RTK-GPS ground truth and simulation-based tracking with identity-annotated ground truth, overcomes the limitations of GNSS-only evaluation at inter-UAV distances below 2 m.

</details>


### [221] [Diversity You Can Actually Measure: A Fast, Model-Free Diversity Metric for Robotics Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11634)
*Sreevardhan Sirigiri,Nathan Samuel de Lara,Christopher Agia,Florian Shkurti,Fabio Ramos*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出FAKTUAL算法，通过基于签名核的熵度量来量化机器人演示数据的多样性，并利用该度量进行数据筛选以提升模仿学习性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人模仿学习的数据集通常包含不同长度的长时程轨迹和高维观测（如RGB视频），难以在尊重轨迹结构和几何特性的前提下量化多样性

Method: 扩展香农熵和冯·诺依曼熵，基于签名核的Gram矩阵定义签名变换熵，提出FAKTUAL算法：通过最大化熵来选择给定预算下的最优演示子集

Result: 在图像和状态基准测试（RoboMimic、MetaWorld）以及四个真实世界操作任务中，FAKTUAL相比随机选择持续提升下游成功率，计算效率显著优于现有方法

Conclusion: 演示数据集的熵是理解和改进机器人模仿学习中数据集多样性的实用工具，FAKTUAL提供了一种简单、模型无关的数据筛选方法

Abstract: Robotics datasets for imitation learning typically consist of long-horizon trajectories of different lengths over states, actions, and high-dimensional observations (e.g., RGB video), making it non-trivial to quantify diversity in a way that respects the underlying trajectory structure and geometry. We extend Shannon and von Neumann entropy to this setting by defining signature transform-based entropy on the Gram matrix of a signature kernel over demonstrations, yielding entropy and diversity metrics that operate directly on the demonstration dataset. Building on these metrics, we study how dataset diversity affects generalization performance in robot imitation learning and propose a simple, model-free way to curate diverse demonstrations. We introduce FAKTUAL (FAst trajectory Kernel enTropy cUration for imitation Learning), a data curation algorithm that selects a subset of demonstrations maximizing entropy given a subset-size budget. FAKTUAL is fully model-free, requires no access to the imitation policy or rollouts, and adds negligible overhead relative to policy training. We evaluate our approach on image and state-based RoboMimic and MetaWorld benchmarks, as well as four real-world manipulation tasks. Across tasks and architectures, diversity-aware curation with FAKTUAL consistently improves downstream success rates over random selection, while being substantially more computationally efficient compared to recent robot data curation methods. Our results suggest that the entropy of demonstration datasets is a practical tool for understanding and improving dataset diversity in robot imitation learning.

</details>


### [222] [Learn Structure, Adapt on the Fly: Multi-Scale Residual Learning and Online Adaptation for Aerial Manipulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11638)
*Samaksh Ujjawal,Naveen Sudheer Nair,Shivansh Pratap Singh,Rishabh Dev Yadav,Wei Pan,Spandan Roy*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Factorized Dynamics Transformer (FDT)和Latent Residual Adapter (LRA)的预测-自适应框架，用于自主空中机械臂的非平稳多尺度残差动力学建模与实时补偿，提升未知负载下的控制性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主空中机械臂(AAMs)具有耦合非线性、非平稳多尺度残差动力学特性，传统解析模型依赖固定参数结构，静态数据驱动模型假设平稳动态，在配置变化和负载变化时性能下降。现有学习架构未显式分解跨变量耦合和多尺度时间效应。

Method: 提出预测-自适应框架：1) Factorized Dynamics Transformer (FDT)将物理变量作为独立token，显式跨变量注意力机制，结构分离短时域惯性依赖和长时域气动效应；2) Latent Residual Adapter (LRA)在潜空间通过递归最小二乘法进行快速线性自适应，保持离线非线性表示；3) 将自适应残差预测集成到残差补偿自适应控制器中。

Result: 在真实空中机械臂上进行未知负载实验，相比最先进学习基线，展示了更高的预测保真度、加速的扰动衰减和优越的闭环跟踪精度，同时保持严格的实时可行性。

Conclusion: 提出的FDT-LRA框架能够有效处理自主空中机械臂的非平稳多尺度残差动力学，通过显式分解跨变量耦合和多尺度时间效应，结合快速在线自适应，实现了在未知负载条件下的高性能实时控制。

Abstract: Autonomous Aerial Manipulators (AAMs) are inherently coupled, nonlinear systems that exhibit nonstationary and multiscale residual dynamics, particularly during manipulator reconfiguration and abrupt payload variations. Conventional analytical dynamic models rely on fixed parametric structures, while static data-driven model assume stationary dynamics and degrade under configuration changes and payload variations. Moreover, existing learning architectures do not explicitly factorize cross-variable coupling and multi-scale temporal effects, conflating instantaneous inertial dynamics with long-horizon regime evolution. We propose a predictive-adaptive framework for real-time residual modeling and compensation in AAMs. The core of this framework is the Factorized Dynamics Transformer (FDT), which treats physical variables as independent tokens. This design enables explicit cross-variable attention while structurally separating short-horizon inertial dependencies from long-horizon aerodynamic effects. To address deployment-time distribution shifts, a Latent Residual Adapter (LRA) performs rapid linear adaptation in the latent space via Recursive Least Squares, preserving the offline nonlinear representation without prohibitive computational overhead. The adapted residual forecast is directly integrated into a residual-compensated adaptive controller. Real-world experiments on an aerial manipulator subjected to unseen payloads demonstrate higher prediction fidelity, accelerated disturbance attenuation, and superior closed-loop tracking precision compared to state-of-the-art learning baselines, all while maintaining strict real-time feasibility.

</details>


### [223] [Chunk-Boundary Artifact in Action-Chunked Generative Policies: A Noise-Sensitive Failure Mechanism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11642)
*Rui Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究发现动作分块策略中的边界不连续问题是一种可分析、可干预的噪声敏感故障机制，而非不可避免的执行缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动作分块已成为生成式视觉运动策略的核心设计选择，但分块边界处出现的执行不连续性尚未得到充分理解。本文旨在深入分析这些边界不连续性的本质和影响。

Method: 在预训练的动作分块策略中，首先识别分块边界伪影与任务失败的关联性；其次在固定观测上下文下，通过改变潜在噪声系统性地调节伪影幅度；最后通过识别噪声空间中的伪影相关方向并应用轨迹级引导，可靠地改变所有评估任务中的伪影幅度。

Result: 分块边界伪影与任务失败显著相关（p < 1e-4），且在推演过程中出现而非事后症状；在固定观测下，改变潜在噪声可系统调节伪影幅度；通过噪声空间干预可可靠改变伪影幅度，在困难任务中成功/失败分布相应变化，在接近上限任务中正面增益被策略饱和压缩而负面因果效应仍然可见。

Conclusion: 研究将边界不连续性从不可避免的执行缺陷重新定义为可分析、噪声主导且可干预的故障机制，为理解和改进动作分块策略提供了新视角。

Abstract: Action chunking has become a central design choice for generative visuomotor policies, yet the execution discontinuities that arise at chunk boundaries remain poorly understood. In a frozen pretrained action-chunked policy, we identify chunk-boundary artifact as a noise-sensitive failure mechanism. First, artifact is strongly associated with task failure (p < 1e-4, permutation test) and emerges during the rollout rather than only as a post-hoc symptom. Second, under a fixed observation context, changing only latent noise systematically modulates artifact magnitude. Third, by identifying artifact-related directions in noise space and applying trajectory-level steering, we reliably alter artifact magnitude across all evaluated tasks. In hard-task settings with remaining outcome headroom, the success/failure distribution shifts accordingly; on near-ceiling tasks, positive gains are compressed by policy saturation, while the negative causal effect remains visible. Overall, we recast boundary discontinuity from an unavoidable execution nuisance into an analyzable, noise-dominated, and intervenable failure mechanism.

</details>


### [224] [A Hybrid Neural-Assisted Unscented Kalman Filter for Unmanned Ground Vehicle Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11649)
*Gal Versano,Itzik Klein*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出混合估计框架，结合经典状态估计与深度学习，用神经网络从原始IMU/GNSS数据预测噪声协方差，实现sim2real训练，在三种不同车辆数据集上位置精度提升12.7%


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统无人地面车辆导航中，固定噪声协方差矩阵难以适应动态真实环境条件，需要更鲁棒的噪声估计方法

Method: 提出混合估计框架，保持无迹卡尔曼滤波基本方程不变，开发专用深度神经网络直接从原始惯性/GNSS测量预测过程和测量噪声不确定性，采用sim2real方法仅使用仿真数据进行训练

Result: 在三种不同车辆（越野车、乘用车、移动机器人）、传感器、路面和环境条件的160分钟测试集上，相比自适应模型方法位置精度提升12.7%

Conclusion: 该方法为无人地面车辆导航提供了可扩展且更鲁棒的解决方案，成功结合经典状态估计与深度学习优势

Abstract: Modern autonomous navigation for unmanned ground vehicles relies on different estimators to fuse inertial sensors and GNSS measurements.
